# Tèo Dub Studio — 28/10/2026

## 28/10/2026 (3) — A link waiting for a download source rests alone; a video Douyin reports deleted is a failure, not a wait (Claude Code)

**Why (owner: "xem hiện tại có lỗi gì không mà sao nó đang chạy thì lại dừng", then "duyệt A,B"):** rotation `cd51c550…` was paused at 16:1x. Link `test`, Video 85 (`7626260130841103652`): Douyin direct answered `filter_detail.filter_reason` for a guest, SaveTik "not found", Viện Phần Mềm timeout, Social Video Download maintenance (to 16:25), AnySave daily quota (to 05:59). `_all_unavailable` turned that into "Chờ nguồn tải … thử lại sau 16:25", the item became `cancelled` with `resume_after`, and `_run_rotation` paused the WHOLE queue (demo/chien_dich_28_10/câu_ca idle) with `test` keeping the turn, so each auto-resume retried the same dead video. A read-only probe through the logged-in channel-scan profile (`probe_logged_in.py`, no download, no cookies read) got `filter_reason: status_deleted` ("因作品权限或已被删除") — the video is gone; being logged in would not help (the downloader never uses that profile on purpose: "no login, no cookies").

**Change (A, `campaign_batch.py`):** a campaign result that is `paused` + message starts with `campaign.WAITING_SOURCE` + numeric `resume_after` (`_source_wait`) no longer ends the rotation: its row becomes `pending` with message and `wait_until = max(resume_after, now + SOURCE_WAIT_MIN)` (60 s: a stale time never makes a busy loop), `next_index = index+1`. `_next_turn` skips rows with `wait_until > now` (plus done/needs_attention/stopped). When no row can take a turn but some wait, the earliest waiting row is set `paused` and the queue pauses with its message (the desktop's `continue_rotation` finds the first `paused` row and calls `batch.resume()` when `auto_resume_due`, unchanged). A fresh `_run_rotation` pass where nothing is selectable only because links wait clears every `wait_until` (an explicit Chạy / Tiếp tục tries now, as before); a link's own Chạy (`_absorb_joins` on its row) pops `wait_until`; a row that starts running pops it. `stop.exists()` (Dừng) and the leave request keep their old branches first; AI quota/login pauses still pause the whole queue. Serial (non-rotate) queues untouched.

**Change (B, `douyin_direct.py` + `browser_download.py`):** `GONE_FILTER = 'status_deleted'` → `ProviderUnavailable(reason='video_gone')` (not in `provider_health.PAUSES`, so Douyin is not paused for the next video; every other `filter_reason` stays `parse_failed`). `_gone_ending(failures)`: at both endings of `resolve_providers` (last provider failed, or the last was skipped), if Douyin direct failed with `video_gone` and no source returned the video, raise `ProviderUnavailable(reason='video_gone')` with a message that neither starts with `WAITING_SOURCE` nor contains the campaign's pause keywords (hạn mức/quota/usage limit/rate limit/đăng nhập/login/credits — asserted by a test) → the item is a normal red `failed`, the campaign goes on, "Thử lại video lỗi" retries it. A later source that does get the video still wins (the gone ending is only the final fallthrough).

**Tests:** new `tests/test_source_wait.py` (14: rotation — other links keep their turns, stale time not retried in the same pass, comes back when due, Chạy/Tiếp tục tries now, own Chạy, Dừng and Dừng link này, other pauses unchanged, single link; provider chain — gone fails for that video without pausing Douyin, also with sites already paused/skipped, another source still wins, non-deleted refusal still waits, a campaign continues to the next video); `test_douyin_direct` +1; `test_campaign_rotation.test_waiting_for_a_download_source_pauses_the_whole_rotation` was rewritten to the new approved behaviour (renamed `…rests_that_link_and_pauses_only_when_every_link_waits`). Related suites (rotation, batch, controller, douyin direct, provider health, download adapter, slow switch, campaign, tracking, campaign UI: 192) pass with `-X utf8`; without it `test_restore_does_not_launch_and_interrupted_status_is_presented_paused` fails on the live code too (cp1252 read, pre-existing, not part of this change). Not run: real Douyin / download sites / browser.

**Not done (owner's option C):** using the logged-in channel profile as a fallback for guest refusals — not needed for deleted videos; the profile and "downloader never logs in" rule are unchanged.

## 28/10/2026 (2) — Optional per-video posting times (`slot_times`), owed slots filled ~15 min after a video is ready (Claude Code)

**Why (owner, after seeing the Page AI panel screenshot):** wanted an explicit HH:MM per video instead of a first-time+gap rhythm, but with no post skipped: "nếu đến đúng giờ mà chưa có video để đăng thì nó sẽ đẩy vào hàng đợi sau khoảng 15 phút sẽ đăng lại". Approved after two rounds ("lưu ý không làm ảnh hưởng đến logic hệ thống, mọi thứ phải giữ nguyên vẹn").

**Change (no AI call, no live side effect):** `page_ai.py` — `OPTIONAL_KEYS` gains `slot_times` (a list of zero-padded `HH:MM` strings, one per `videos_per_day`, strictly ascending, no duplicates); `SLOT_KEYS` (`slot_start`/`slot_gap`) and `slot_times` are mutually exclusive (`_check_slots`), validated by new `_check_slot_times`. `fixed_slots` (non-auto scheduled-AI mode) and the new `_auto_slots_rhythm` (renamed from the old `auto_slots` body) are unchanged; `auto_slots` now dispatches to `_auto_slots_custom` when `slot_times` is set. `_auto_slots_custom`: a time still ahead of `clock+AUTO_LEAD_SECONDS` keeps its own appointment; a time already passed today with nothing scheduled for it is "owed" and is filled at `begin` (`math.ceil(clock)+AUTO_LEAD_SECONDS`, whole seconds — see pitfall below), owed times spaced `AUTO_DEBT_GAP_SECONDS` (900 s = 15 min) apart so a backlog doesn't post all at once; same daily quota, same-minute, and `GONE_STATES` rules as the rhythm. `_owner_times` factors the per-day (hour, minute) list for `fixed_slots` (the owner's `slot_times`, or the rhythm expanded to `videos_per_day` — behaviour-preserving refactor, covered by the unchanged `test_page_ai_slots`/`test_page_ai_auto` suites passing verbatim). `public_snapshot` exports `slot_times` like the other slot keys.

**Pitfall found in testing:** the first draft returned `begin=clock+AUTO_LEAD_SECONDS` (a sub-second float) as the owed slot's own due_at. `context()`'s `make_prompt`/`validate_plan` round-trip `due_at` through `isoformat(timespec='seconds')`, which truncates the fraction; the truncated stamp then failed the window's `start <= stamp` check (since `start` itself keeps the fraction), so the AI's plan was refused with "Giờ AI chọn ngoài khoảng kế hoạch đã cấu hình." and nothing was armed — reproduced with two new `AutoControllerCustomTimesTests`. Fixed by rounding the owed slot up to a whole second (`math.ceil`), never earlier than the lead would otherwise allow. The existing rhythm mode was never exposed to this because rhythm stamps are always whole-minute-aligned.

**UI (`page_ai_ui.py`):** new `Checkbutton` "Đặt giờ riêng cho từng video" next to "Video mỗi ngày", with an `Entry` for a comma-separated list; `PageAIPanel.slot_times_values()` parses/validates/sorts (mirrors `_check_slot_times`, reusing `clock_text` for zero-padding), `toggle_custom_times()` greys the rhythm `Entry`s (`slot_start_entry`/`slot_gap_entry`, now named) while it is ticked and the reverse; `slot_values()` returns `None` when custom times are on; `describe_slots()`/`save()`/`load()` branch on `custom_times_on` before falling back to the unchanged rhythm path. Unticked (default), the saved config has no `slot_times` key and behaves exactly as before — covered by `test_the_box_is_visible_and_off_by_default_and_the_old_rhythm_fields_stay_enabled`/`test_unticked_behaves_exactly_like_before_the_feature`.

**Tests:** new `tests/test_page_ai_custom_times.py` (26: validation, `fixed_slots`/`auto_slots` custom-times unit tests, the round-trip pitfall above, `public_snapshot`) and `tests/test_page_ai_custom_times_ui.py` (9: box visibility/default, enable/disable wiring, save/sort/reload, mismatch/duplicate/malformed refusals, untick-restores-rhythm). All fail on the live code (`run_old.py`, 35 failures/errors). Full related suite (`test_page_ai_slots`, `test_page_ai_auto`, `test_page_ai_auto_ui`, `test_page_ai`, `test_page_ai_hidden`, `test_page_ai_captions`, `test_page_content_plan`, `test_plan_review_ui`) passes unchanged, run 3× to rule out the timing flakiness this feature's clock arithmetic invites.

## 28/10/2026 — Listen again to the empty stretches with a better model; burned captions stay until the speaker finishes (Claude Code)

**Why (owner: "kiểm tra xem hệ thống có dịch thiếu / phụ đề thiếu / thuyết minh thiếu", then "duyệt kế hoạch, không làm ảnh hưởng logic hệ thống"):** Video 1 of chien_dich_28_10 (the one posted to Demo 23-10 at 11:00, 6:19): 124 Chinese cues → 124 Vietnamese → 124 voices, none muted, so translation and narration were complete *for the recognized lines*. The loss is in recognition: GTX 1050 (2 GB) makes `recognizer_runtime('auto')` pick Whisper **small** (all 211 jobs since 20/10 did). Re-listening to the 13 stretches without a Vietnamese caption with Whisper medium (CPU, `probes` in the session scratchpad, read-only) found real speech in ≥ 5 of them (0:34–0:52 the opening line, 4:23 "这是鲨鱼", 0:58, 1:30, 1:40), plus runs of one repeated phrase (4:00, 5:57) that look like hallucination. Separately a burned caption lasts only as long as the Vietnamese voice (at 2:18 the Chinese line runs 10 s, the voice 1.8 s, so nothing shows at 2:24). The Chinese "响螺" in the owner's screenshot is the video's own burned text, never translated.

**Change (no AI call):** `asr.transcribe` — after the normal pass, only when `asr_model == 'auto'` and the model used was `small`, `_recheck_gaps` cuts each `empty_stretches(cues, duration)` (≥ `GAP_MIN_S` 4 s, both ends included, at most `GAP_MAX_TOTAL_S` 600 s of audio) from the source with the bundled ffmpeg and runs `GAP_MODEL` (medium, device/precision from `recognizer_runtime('medium')`) with the same options (vad, zh). Lines are clipped to their stretch, filtered by `_keep_recovered` (no_speech ≤ 0.6, avg_logprob ≥ −1.0, compression ≤ 2.4, not one 1–4 character unit repeated ≥ 3 times, the same text < 3 times per video) and merged in time order; the first model is freed first. The saved `.asr.json` gets `gap_recheck` (model, stretches, listened, audio_s, added, dropped, status or the failure message); `model`/`requested_model` stay the first pass. Any error except `Cancelled` keeps the first recognition (status `failed`). `render` — `_display_ends(final_cues, clean_cues, speed, duration)`: a burned caption ends at max(voice end, min(speaker's source end / speed, start + `DISPLAY_MAX_MS` 6000, next line's start, video end)); applied to the placed boxes before `write_captions`, and for `box == 'none'` to a separate `work/display.srt` read by the filter. `final.srt`, `render-plan.json` cues, voice placement, covers and `_verify` are untouched.

**Tests:** new `tests/test_listen_gaps.py` (14: stretch finding, filtering, merge order and clipping, one model without a stretch, a chosen model respected, failures only noted, Dừng stops, cache reuse), `test_render` +4 (`_display_ends` rules, speed, real renders: ASS ends longer while final.srt/plan stay, display.srt for no box). All fail on the live code (`run_new_tests_on_old_code.py`). Real Video 1 (local Whisper only, no AI): `preview-asr*.out` — the first pass varies from run to run (111–130 lines; the live app saved 124), the re-listen found 8–9 empty stretches of ≥ 4 s (~80 s of audio) and added 3–8 lines: always the 0:34–0:52 opening ("今天我们带了两条… 把这个三百平的金石壳给它抽掉"), plus short cries (呼/快走/哎呦, 哇, 一二三) — a few are probably noise, `gap_recheck.added_lines` lists them for review. Findings that shaped the filters: Whisper's own VAD removed that real opening line in the second pass (so `vad_filter=False` there), real short cries score avg_logprob ≈ −1.05 (threshold −1.1), noise came back as "YES SIR", "二撒 tones", "完蛋了"×4 / "一二三"×2 (English word, no Chinese character, same line twice in a row and one phrase repeated are dropped). Time: 97–281 s per video in total (the first run after a cold start took 684 s once: not reproduced; medium loads in ~4 s when warm). Captions: `preview/video1-truoc` vs `video1-sau` (rendered from the job's own speech plan and voice clips, same 124 lines; 153 s / 112 s), `preview/so-sanh-2-24.png`: at 2:21 and 2:24 the Vietnamese line (voice 2:18–2:20) is still shown, gone by 2:27 (6 s cap). Known limit: the box is fitted to the original caption at the line's start, so a different Chinese caption appearing later can show past its edge.

**Why (owner, two screenshots, then "duyệt kế hoạch"):** chien_dich_28_10's finished Video 1 was not scheduled for Demo 23-10. All 6 fail on the live code (`run_new_tests_on_old_code.py`). Previews `preview/truoc-*.png` / `sau-*.png`. Evidence `work/20261028-input-notices/`.

## 26/10/2026 — Page switch survives Facebook's reload; bounded tab checks; Tèo closes its own finished post tabs; stuck-Chrome notice (Claude Code)

**Why (owner: "có lỗi này, hãy kiểm tra thật kỹ nguyên nhân", then "duyệt kế hoạch cho phần A,B,C,D"):** 26/10 10:30 both Pages of account 0b1f0b7f were due. Phú thọ (test Video 38, post 60f78049) switched the actor du lịch toàn cầu → Phú thọ through the menu; the switch went through (10:40 read-only capture: "Select profile" shows Phú thọ ticked, "Comment as Phú thọ"), then the attempt failed before typing/upload/Publish with the raw "Page.evaluate: Execution context was destroyed, most likely because of a navigation" (Facebook replaces the document after the choice; `_safe`'s `tab.evaluate(AUTH_SCRIPT)` ran during it; reproduced offline in `probes/crash_probe2.py`). du lịch toàn cầu 10:30 (38f14272, queued behind it) opened its menu and "Select profile" at 10:30:15, then nothing for 20 minutes: the desktop watchdog (`VIDEO_PREPARATION_SECONDS`) failed the schedule at 10:50:15, but the post stayed `preparing` (its generator still blocked in a Playwright call) and the 10:35 request then sat "Đã nhận yêu cầu" until its own 20-minute timeout at 11:10 — every later post of both Pages queues behind it until Tèo's Chrome is closed. Cause underneath: every prepare opens its own tab and none is closed (19 since Chrome started 08:41 → 21 tabs, 7.6 GB working set of the PC's 15.8 GB, 1.4 GB free, one tab "Crashed"); `_can_switch` reads every tab with `count()`/`is_visible()`, which have no time limit (a tab that does not answer holds them for as long as it does: `probes/crash_probe.py`, `crash_probe4.py`; a crashed tab fails at once with "Target crashed": `crash_probe3.py`). py-spy (installed in the session scratchpad only) showed the browse thread inside the Playwright loop; the exact blocked call is not observable (greenlet), the screen state is just before the profile row click, where `_can_switch` runs. Evidence: `work/20261026-page-switch-hang/investigation.json`, `teo-chrome-1040.png`.

**Change (owner-approved A–D):**
- A `facebook_web_post`: `_post_tabs(context)` records the tabs a prepare, a verify or a result read opens (`_remember_tab`, with the attempt number). At each prepare's start `_close_finished_tabs` closes those whose attempt is finished — a newer attempt exists, state `draft`/`failed`, or `unknown`/`published` with `browser_tracking.submitted_at` ≥ `FINISHED_TAB_SECONDS` (3 min) ago — and only when `open_editor(tab)` is False (no composer). Kept: review, preparing/submitting/processing, unknown without a submission, any tab showing an editor or not answering, every tab Tèo did not open (the session tab, user tabs). Its binding is dropped with it. Failures there never stop the post.
- B `facebook_page_identity`: `_steady(tab, read, deadline)` re-reads after a navigation-interrupted error (`_navigation_interrupted`) once `_reloading` waited for domcontentloaded; used for every read in `_selector` (also `dialog.evaluate(CHOICES_SCRIPT)`, now bounded by the deadline). After the profile row click (a navigation error from the click itself = dispatched, never repeated) the wait loop also reads `performance.timeOrigin`: it ends when the menu is closed and the document was replaced, or after `SWITCH_RELOAD_SECONDS` (5) with no reload. `facebook_web_post._page_ready` = the two `_open_composer` loops, tolerant the same way (`_safe_page`'s own refusals still stop at once); the post-switch `goto` is repeated if Facebook's reload interrupts it. Nothing is clicked again.
- C `open_editor(tab, seconds=TAB_CHECK_SECONDS)` (3): bounded `locator('html').wait_for(attached)` first, then the composer check; crashed/closed → False, no answer → None. `_can_switch` skips crashed tabs and refuses at once on None ("Một tab Facebook trong Chrome của Tèo không phản hồi sau 3 giây…").
- D `facebook_ui`: when the watchdog expires a request the worker had started (running/verifying), `session.stalled` remembers it; any later message for that request (or the session ending) clears it. After `STALLED_NOTICE_SECONDS` (60) every queued request of that session and the status show `STALLED_MESSAGE` (close that account's Tèo Chrome; later schedules reopen it). Queued requests keep their state and deadline.

**Tests:** `test_facebook_page_identity` +6 (`reloading_fixture(delay)`: reload after the choice at 0/150/400 ms; reload + prepare + one publish; a busy tab stops the switch in < 8 s with no choice; a crashed tab does not block; finished post tabs (published ≥ 3 min, failed) close at the next prepare while the user tab stays; a recent tab, one showing an editor and a review tab stay); `test_browser_schedule_integration` +2 (stuck notice after 60 s on the queued post, cleared by the late error; a never-started timeout is not "stuck"). All 8 fail on the live code (`run_new_tests_on_old_code.py`). Reload sweep (`sweep_reload.py`, 38 runs, reload 0–2000 ms after the choice): live 15/38 fail (25–200 ms: "Chưa đọc được danh tính…", menu 0 mục, click timeout), mirror 38/38 OK, the profile chosen once in every run, ≤ 2.7 s. `test_facebook_page_identity` (19 tests) takes ~190 s in the mirror: each switch in a fixture without a reload waits the 5 s.

**Not done:** the tech-debt trigger "next publisher fix: extract DOM readers" — out of the approved scope; the publisher is now ~2,440 lines.

## 24/10/2026 (afternoon) — A link tab shows its Chạy / Dừng link này at once; why two Pages stopped posting (Claude Code; deployed 16:34 with the section above)

## 24/10/2026 (midday) — Thử lại video lỗi while links take turns (Claude Code; deployed 11:29 after the owner's "duyệt cài")

**Why:** after the dub fixes the owner asked which button runs both links in turns and their failed videos too; none did (rotation turns skip failed videos; a link tab's Thử lại video lỗi ran that link alone and only when nothing ran). Owner approved the plan ("duyệt kế hoạch").

**How:** `campaign.request_retry(directory)` (desktop) writes `retry-<uuid>.json` with the numbers failed now (none failed → no file). The worker, the only writer of campaign.json, reads them at the start of `_run_campaign_locked` (`requested_retries`), marks the listed items that are still failed `retry_once: true`, saves the manifest, then deletes the files. `should_process` treats a marked failed item like a pending one; the mark is popped when the video starts, so it gets one try (fails again → plain failed, skipped). `CampaignBatchController.retry_link` = request + `run_link` (join the running rotation / restart a stopped one / new rotation; 'already' when the link runs on its own → the marks apply at its next run). Link tab: the button is enabled when the link has failed videos not yet asked for and no single video/login runs, also while links run; rows show `campaign_tracking.RETRYING` "Chờ thử lại" (amber tag `retrying`) for marked items and pending requests; status "Lỗi N (chờ thử lại M)"; with every failed video asked for, state `retrying` ("chờ thử lại" / "Chờ thử lại video lỗi · tới lượt link này là làm") replaces `attention` and the red notice is blank. Unchanged: Chạy skips failed videos (no automatic retry or Codex use); the '＋ Thêm link' tab's Thử lại video lỗi (standalone `--retry-failed`); stops; a waiting-source pause keeps the marks.

**Tests:** new `tests/test_campaign_retry_turns.py` (7: retried videos first in the link's turns while the other keeps its turns — A1 B1 A2 B2 A3; failing again → one try only; Chạy alone still skips them and only videos failed at the press are marked; a stopped link keeps its marks for its next Chạy; a waiting download source keeps the other marks; malformed/odd request files ignored and consumed; the "Chờ thử lại" label); `test_campaign_link_tabs` (retry with nothing running now starts the rotation with the request; new: retry while another link runs joins the turns, no spawn). All 9 fail on the live code (`run_new_tests_on_old_code.py`). Campaign suites in the mirror: 221 OK apart from the known flaky `test_the_later_of_stop_and_run_wins`. Real batch worker smoke (`smoke_retry_turns.py`, throwaway data, per-video child stubbed to fail): mirror A1 B1 A2 B2, requests consumed, no marks left, locks free; live code B1 B2 only (request ignored). Screenshots `preview/truoc.png` (button disabled while test runs) / `preview/sau.png` (after pressing: 7 × "Chờ thử lại", tab "demo · 18/30 · chờ thử lại").

Preservation 11:21:38 → 11:29:25: 30,524 files and schedules unchanged, previous events kept; 2 new events + 1 post `attempt` 0 → 1 / failed = the app's own 11:25 scheduled post for "du lịch toàn cầu" ("Không xác định duy nhất nút Chuyển ngay trên đúng Page (0 mục)", before Publish; schedule needs_attention), the same error as its 10:50 post. Live suite (`related-summary-live.json`, finished 14:25; the PC slept 11:35–14:21 per the Power-Troubleshooter event, hence 10,522 s): 2,376 tests, only the pre-existing `test_campaign_capture_freezes_mix_*` (3). ## 24/10/2026 — Three dub fixes: exact-length faster lines, recognized last line ends with the picture, saved models without the Hub (Claude Code; deployed 10:20 after the owner's "duyệt cài")

**Why (owner asked for the causes of the 23/10 failures, then "duyệt kế hoạch"):** 9 failed videos on campaigns test (`ffb381c5…`) and demo (`99a8996a…`): 6 × "Thời lượng câu đọc sau khi đọc nhanh không khớp" (test 2, 12; demo 1, 12, 18, 20), demo 9 "SRT dài hơn video đã chọn", test 15 `httpx.ReadError [WinError 10054]` while VieNeu loaded, test 18 all five download sources failed 22 times (not a code fix). Scan of all 507 jobs' `error.log`: the stretch error appears only since the 23/10 dub-sync deploy. Each of the 6 stopped on a short line (1.38–1.95 s) needing ~1.03×.

**1 — `render._stretch_clip`:** atempo (2048-sample window at 48 kHz) keeps back up to about half a window at EOF: 120 real lines × 24 tempos → output 3–22 ms short (median 15.5 ms; up to ~32 ms on a synthetic syllable signal), and `abs(written-expected) > max(20 ms, 1%)` failed short lines. Now the line is followed by `STRETCH_PAD_S` .1 s of silence, the output is buffered and cut to exactly `round(frames/tempo)`; refused (same message) if it is shorter than that (the line's end would be lost) or longer than (frames+pad)/tempo + max(20 ms, 1%) (not sped up). `sweep_real_lines.py`: live 92/2880 runs fail, 0 exact; staged 0 fail, 2880/2880 exact. Faster lines are now 7–21 ms longer (their own end, previously cut); later starts move ≤ 12 ms; tempos unchanged.

**2 — `pipeline._end_with_picture`:** for ASR + Codex cues only (not a supplied SRT), a cue ending more than 200 ms past the picture but starting inside it gets `end_ms = floor(duration_s*1000)`; applied in `captions()` before the unchanged check and again after wording re-reads the adapted SRT. Files on disk are not changed (`vietnamese.srt` keeps Whisper's 2:46.300; the video is 165.867 s). A cue starting at/after the picture end still fails with the old message; supplied SRTs are unchanged. Cues within +200 ms are untouched, so in-progress speech plans keep matching. Retrying demo 9 reuses its translation (`translate_chinese_srt` verifies the existing output; no Codex call).

**3 — `models.saved_first(load)`:** sets `huggingface_hub.constants.HF_HUB_OFFLINE = True` for the load (huggingface_hub's request hook then refuses every Hub request before the network; downloads answer from the cache) and restores it; only `LocalEntryNotFoundError`/`OfflineModeIsEnabled` (a file not saved) → the old online load, once. Used by `tts.get_model` (VieNeu v3turbo/v3nano) and `asr.transcribe` (WhisperModel); `voice_learning._recognizer` unchanged. Root cause: huggingface_hub 1.33 falls back to the cache only on `httpx.ConnectError`/`TimeoutException`, not `ReadError` (reset while reading). Model loads run in a worker process (one job), so the module-level flag touches nothing else. Real check (`verify_saved_models.py`, Hub endpoint a closed port, transport refusing): 0 Hub requests, 16 lookups all offline (15 saved files; `voices_v3_turbo.json` is not on the Hub — skipped by VieNeu online too), engine with denoiser, one line read (1.36 s, 48 kHz); Whisper small/medium/large-v3 resolve to their saved snapshots.

**Tests:** `test_render` +2 (exact length for 1.2 s@1.05, 1.95 s@1.04, 0.9 s@1.021, 1.46 s@1.25 with a syllable-like `syllable_clip`; too little pad → refused), `test_pipeline` +4 (last line ends with the picture, within 200 ms unchanged, starting after the end still stops, supplied SRT still stops), `test_wording_pipeline` +1, new `test_saved_models` (6: a Hub reset (`httpx.ReadError` 10054 at the transport) stops a saved model today; with `saved_first` no request; a missing file goes online; other errors not retried; VieNeu and Whisper are built offline). The new tests fail on the live code with the production messages (`run_new_tests_on_old_code.py`). Stage focused 24 modules 462 tests; mirror full related suite 2,353 tests: only the known `test_campaign_capture_freezes_mix_*` (3) + the 4 needing the real `.venv`; the campaign UI/rotation/link-tab/controller errors of the first mirror run came from a missing placeholder `mirror/.venv/Scripts/python.exe` (220 tests rerun with it: only those 4). Evidence `work/20261024-dub-fit-fixes/`.

**Preview (no AI, `preview_fix.py`):** test video 2 with the live code fails as in production; with the staged code it renders (QC pass, 164.27 s, 41 lines; line 30 "Đảo nhanh trên lửa lớn." at 1:50.8, tempo 1.028). Test video 1 re-rendered vs the owner's real output: same tempos, 16 faster lines 7–21 ms longer, max start shift 12 ms, same duration 178.53 s (`preview/compare-video1.json`).

**Deploy record:** the owner stopped both links ("Dừng link này"; rotation queue paused 10:15, test video 28 and demo video 26 `cancelled` with steps kept); no worker process. Copied 10:20:51 (`deployed.json` = staged = mirror-tested, 12 files). Preservation 10:17:37 → 10:21:03: 30,343 data files, schedules and posts unchanged, events 543 → 543. Live suite 10:31 (`related-summary-live.json`): 2,353 tests, only the pre-existing `test_campaign_capture_freezes_mix_*` (3 entries). Worker modules only: the next worker process loads them; no safe restart needed. Not live-verified yet: the owner's "Thử lại video lỗi" per link is the first real run (expect test 2, 12, 23 and demo 1, 12, 18, 20, 22 to render; test 15 to load VieNeu offline; demo 9 to pass captions).

**Still failing by design — demo video 21 (found at deploy time):** audio and picture are both 139.37 s, but Whisper's last four cues are evenly spaced 2 s slots that drift past the end (2:18.22–2:20.22 starts inside → fixed; 2:20.22–2:22.22 "却最是勾人" starts after the picture → still "SRT dài hơn video" as approved). Real speech, drifted timestamps. Approved ("có sửa") and done as the follow-up in the section above. Link buttons: Chạy (`run_link`: join a running rotation / hand-over after a standalone campaign's video / reuse a stopped or paused rotation that has the link / new rotation with only this link), Dừng link này (`stop_link`: leave request + the link's own stop.request if its video is in progress; standalone → its stop.request (+ leave so a waiting hand-over still starts); not running → row `stopped` directly; in a serial list → its stop.request as before), Thử lại video lỗi (only when nothing runs; routes through `CampaignTab.start(retry=True)` so restart tracking is unchanged), folders, Đóng tab, double-click opens the output or the line's message. `link_state` reads queue.json + the campaign tab's own process + `studio.campaign_busy` (no lock probes).

**Runner.** `campaign_batch`: entry status `stopped` (rotation only), `LINK_STOPPED`, `remove_from_rotation` (`leave-*.json`), requests applied in mtime order (`_requests`), a join for an existing non-running row brings it back to `pending`; a turn that ends paused while that link has a leave request (and no queue stop) marks it `stopped` and the others go on; no active rows left with some `stopped` → queue `paused` ("Không còn link nào đang chạy"). Queue stop (Dừng in '＋ Thêm link' / Dừng hàng đợi) still pauses everything together. `complete_handoff` also starts the turns when the hand-over source was stopped on its own tab. `CampaignTab.display` treats a `stopped` row as out of the turns; `resume_rotation` rejoins it.

## 23/10/2026 (afternoon) — Campaigns in turns ("xen kẽ"): one video per campaign, A → B → A… (Claude Code; deployed 14:43)

**Owner request (approved "duyệt"):** while a campaign runs, still read a new list and create a campaign; the new campaign runs in turns with the running one (one video each, then the next campaign), not after it. Everything else unchanged: one video at a time, same pipeline per video, numbering/names/folders, posting.

**Worker side.** `campaign._run_campaign_locked(..., max_videos=None, verify_done=True)`: with `max_videos=1` the campaign's turn ends before starting its next video → manifest `paused` + `ROTATION_WAIT`, return value is `dict(manifest, turn_over=True)` (not stored). `verify_done=False` skips re-checking finished videos (output hashes, Drive resume) on later turns of the same rotation process; a campaign's first turn checks them as before. A campaign running on its own (no `external_stop`) hands over before its next video when `<campaign>/rotation.request` (`ROTATION_REQUEST`) exists; under a supervisor the file is ignored. `campaign_batch`: queues may carry `order: 'rotate'` + `next_index` (+ `handoff_from` while waiting); serial queues are saved without these keys and run by the untouched loop. `_run_rotation`: absorbs `join-*.json` (written by `add_to_rotation`; the worker is the only queue.json writer), picks the next row not completed/needs_attention from `next_index`, runs one turn, `turn_over` → row pending (`TURN_WAIT`), completed/needs_attention → leaves the rotation, any pause (Dừng, waiting source, quota, login, error) → the whole rotation pauses and the interrupted campaign keeps its turn.

**Desktop.** `CampaignTab`: Lấy danh sách / Tạo chiến dịch mới enabled while campaign work runs (not while a single video, login or scan runs); creating then asks (askyesno) and `join_running` → `CampaignBatchController.join`: running rotation → join file; standalone campaign → new rotation queue `[running, new]`, `next_index` 1, `handoff_from`, `rotation.request` written; serial list running → only saved. `poll` → `continue_rotation`: `complete_handoff` spawns the batch worker once the handed-over campaign is no longer running and shows `ROTATION_WAIT`/done (stopped for another reason → queue paused for the owner); a paused rotation whose paused campaign is `auto_resume_due` resumes itself (once a minute). Chạy / Tiếp tục on a campaign of a ready/paused rotation resumes the rotation (`resume_rotation`); a standalone start deletes a stale `rotation.request`. Dừng stops the rotation (batch stop); Dừng while reading a list only stops the reading if campaigns run. Labels: "Chạy xen kẽ · chờ tới lượt", "Chờ chạy xen kẽ (sau video đang làm)". `CampaignLibraryTab`: order combobox `ORDERS` ("Lần lượt từng chiến dịch" default / "Xen kẽ từng video"). Known edge: if the app is closed while a hand-over waits, the rotation starts when Tèo is reopened (README says so).

**Tests:** new `tests/test_campaign_rotation.py` (24: turn end/hand-over only between videos/ignored under a supervisor, order A1 B1 C1 A2 B2 A3, join while running, stop + resume keeps the turn, failures continue, waiting source pauses all, first-turn-only verification, serial queue keys unchanged, field validation, controller join/handoff/complete/stopped, tab buttons/ask/resume/Dừng while reading/labels, library order). Existing campaign suites unchanged and passing (177 in mirror, same as the live code). Mirror related suite 2,326 tests: same pre-existing failures as before (`test_campaign_capture_freezes_mix_*` x3 + 4 needing the real `.venv`). Real batch worker smoke (`smoke_worker.py`, throwaway data, finished/failed videos only): rotation ends needs_attention as expected, locks free. Evidence `work/20261023-campaign-rotation/`.

**Deploy record:** the owner pressed Dừng on video-mon-an at 14:06 (video 16 cancelled, steps kept; 15/100 done); no worker process. Copied 14:43 (`deployed.json` hashes = staged = mirror-tested). Preservation 14:40:06 → 14:43:20: data files, schedules and posts unchanged, events 454 → 454. Live suite 15:13 (`related-summary-live.json`): 2,326 tests, only the pre-existing `test_campaign_capture_freezes_mix_*` (3 entries). The desktop must be safe-restarted to load the UI part (campaign_ui, campaign_library_ui, campaign_batch_controller); worker modules load fresh per run. Not live-verified yet: the first real rotation is the owner creating a campaign while video-mon-an runs.

## 23/10/2026 (morning) — Dub lines fit the picture, ducking, v3 translation length rule (Claude Code; deployed after the owner listened to the preview: "DUYỆT CÀI")

**Trigger (owner asked whether the dub is natural and accurate):** `work/20261023-dub-quality-check/` (`measure_dub.py`, `summary.json`): translation good, numbers read correctly, but sync broken — the Vietnamese clip is longer than its original line in 57–67% of lines (median +0.46 s) and `render` placed lines back to back (`start = max(target, cursor+gap)`), so video 15 drifted to median 11.7 s / max 56 s late, video 14 had the whole picture slowed to 0.918x (lines 33–41 s early mid-video), video 12 +4 to −12 s; the Chinese voice at 56% was about as loud as the dub (−1.4 dB median). Owner approved A, B, C, D (max reading speed 1.25x).

**A — fit each line to its moment (`render.render_video`):** target = cue start / speed; on time → start at the target, behind → `max(target, cursor + CATCH_UP_GAP_MS 80)`; room = next target − gap (last line: picture end) − start; `_fit_tempo` → 1 if it fits (≤ `FIT_TOLERANCE` 1.02 over), else `min(MAX_FIT_TEMPO 1.25, frames/room)`; `_stretch_clip` runs FFmpeg's atempo in-process (PyAV `av.filter.Graph`, bit-identical to the CLI, checked) on the normalized 48 kHz stereo clip; probes, narration and cues use the stretched clip. `effective_speed = speed` (no uniform slowdown); overrun → `tpad` clone holds the last frame (`last_frame_held_s`). `_caption_boxes(…, end_s, …)` reads a line spoken over the held frame from the picture's last 0.5 s. Plan: per-clip `tempo`, `line_fit`, `last_frame_held_s`. Simulation on the real videos (`work/20261023-dub-quality-check/simulate_fit.py`): median lateness 0.0–0.1 s, p90 1.0–2.0 s, max 3.9–5.0 s (was 14–56 s).

**C — ducking:** `_duck_envelope` writes the original sound's gain as raw mono f32 at `ENVELOPE_RATE` 500 Hz: 1 in pauses, `DUCK_GAIN/original_gain` under the voice (absolute 12%), raised-cosine fades `DUCK_DOWN_S` .15 before / `DUCK_UP_S` .3 after, pauses < `DUCK_HOLD_S` .6 stay low; one spare second because `amultiply` ends with its shorter input. `_original_audio` builds the same chain (`…atrim[loud];[N:a:0]aresample,pan=stereo|c0=c0|c1=c0[duck];[loud][duck]amultiply`) for the export and `_expected_audio_mix`, so the existing RMS/per-probe mix QC still compares identical graphs (two encodes are byte-identical). `pan` avoids the −3 dB default mono upmix. Off when original_gain ≤ .12, voice_gain 0 or no source audio. Plan key `ducking` (or null).

**B — translation length:** `PROMPT_VERSION = 'zh-vi-context-v3'` = v2 + `_DUBBING_LENGTH` (~4 syllables/s, cut filler not meaning; examples); payload rows gain `seconds` (`_spoken_seconds`: until the next cue starts, at most `SPOKEN_TAIL_MS` 1 s after the end, ≥ 0.1) only for `TIMED_PROMPT_VERSIONS`. v1 and v2 (`STYLE_PROMPT_VERSION`) unchanged (test pins v2's SHA-256); jobs keep the version in their `request.json`, so a v2 job in progress finishes with v2.

**D — preview (no AI):** `preview_dub.py` on video 15's first 180 s (82 lines, same speech plan and voice clips). The live code on a 3-minute cut slows the picture to 0.90x (`truoc`), unlike the real 45-minute video (1.0x), so the owner's real output cut to 3:00 is the "before". Before → after: lateness median 17.3 → 0.3 s, max 24.4 → 2.5 s, lines > 1 s 99% → 17%, > 3 s 94% → 0%; voice over the original under it −0.8 → +13 dB; 83% of lines faster (63% at 1.25x); reading speed 3.6 → 4.4 syllables/s (p90 5.2); output RMS −3 dB (original ducked), peak 0.77; QC passed (82 mix checks, correlation 1.0). Frames `preview/kiem-tra-khung.png`. Seen, not caused here: two-line English+Chinese captions (~1:41) are only half covered; "人妖" translated as "bé gái".

**Tests:** `test_render` (+7: fit/catch-up pause, dense talk < 0.5 s late, `_fit_tempo`, pitch kept, ducking levels .56/.12/.56 measured at 170 Hz, gain curve, held-frame intervals; two old tests rewritten for the new rule), `test_audio_mix` (extended-picture test now expects the held frame + ducking), `test_translation_prompts` (+4). Stage: render/audio-mix 41, translation 57 OK; mirror 2,302 tests with the same pre-existing failures as the previous change (`test_campaign_capture_freezes_mix_*` x3 + 4 needing the real `.venv`).

**Deploy record:** copied 10:30:12 with no worker process (all campaigns paused/ready/completed/needs_attention; du-lich-video paused by the owner, `stop.request` 09:18); `deployed.json` hashes = staged = mirror-tested. Preservation 10:30:08 → 10:30:17: 25,197 data files, schedules, posts unchanged, events 385 → 385. Live suite 10:39 (`related-summary-live.json`): 2,302 tests, only the pre-existing `test_campaign_capture_freezes_mix_*` (3 entries). Takes effect in the next worker process (campaign Chạy/Tiếp tục or a single video); already rendered/posted videos are unchanged; a translation in progress keeps its prompt version. Not live-verified yet: the next real render is the first; check its `render-plan.json` (`line_fit`, `ducking`, `last_frame_held_s`) and listen.

## 22/10/2026 (15:48) — Stroke detector + always-centred caption box (Claude Code; deployed during the campaign's own source-wait pause)

**Trigger:** owner's reel of video 10 (Bromo, re-rendered with the 11:00 code): boxes too wide and off-centre. Measured on the output (`work/20261022-ridge-centred-box/research/measure_output.py`): text centred in its box, box 2.3–3.6x the text and 26–114 px left of centre. Cause (`research/mask-718-853.png`): a still scene (tripod) — the old `_mask` (light + local contrast) also marks the light side of step edges, so a checkerboard table, a snack bag and a cup on the caption rows joined the caption; cách A then moved the box with that region.

**Change (owner approved "1 và 2"):** `caption_cover._mask` keeps a light pixel only if it is darker by `CONTRAST` on BOTH sides within `STROKE_REACH` (width/100, 9 px at 960) across or along (1-D min filters with origins; a ridge, not a step). `caption_layout.place`: horizontally always centred, `half_w = max(text, cx-x0, x1-cx)`; vertical `_axis` (cách A) unchanged. Rejected prototypes: solid-run filter (no effect), column-density rule (worse on one line), temporal persistence (camera shake, long captions) — see `research/`.

**Evidence:** 7 real videos rescanned (`research/scans_ridge.json` vs deployed `scans_v2.json` + `scans_live_v10.json`, `compare_ridge.py`): learned caption rows unchanged; centre offset p90 88->13 (video 10), 80->10, 78->17, 92->43, 106->62, 58->35, 1->1; boxes >1.5x text 29.5->18.2%, 20.6->14.5, 17.6->10.5, 30.1->20.2, 21.5->17.4, 17.3->14.8, 14.5->14.5. Coverage sheets `research/check-*.png` (48 frames, largest shrink): every visible caption covered (a 1-character '嗯' narrower than 3% is no candidate but sits inside the centred box). Known limit: shiny pot rims (thin bright ridges) still merge; the centred box is then wider. Tests: `test_caption_cover.StrokeMaskTests` (unit + still-scene video with a white tray beside the caption), `test_caption_layout` centred cases; mirror 1,294 tests (only the known pre-existing `test_campaign_capture_freezes_mix_*` and 4 `.venv` tests); live suite + preservation in `work/20261022-ridge-centred-box/`.

**Open:** video 15 failed "Ghi chú bản dịch Codex không khớp câu nguồn." (Codex's first answer had an invalid review note; not the repair path — no "hỏi lại" in the log). Proposed to the owner: drop invalid notes instead of stopping (no AI call) — awaiting approval.

## 22/10/2026 — Sleep-safe local limits, one Codex follow-up for unusable lines, one-box burned captions (Claude Code; deployed 11:00)

Deployed together at 11:00 with the campaign paused by the owner (no worker process; `deploy.py` refuses otherwise). Backups: `work/20261022-sleep-and-line-repair/before/`, `work/20261022-single-caption-box/before/`; preservation `work/20261022-single-caption-box/preservation.json` (20,356 data files, schedules/posts/events unchanged).

**Incidents (owner asked 22/10):** video 10 failed `[WinError 32]` — Windows was put to sleep from the Start menu 21/10 17:34:43 and woke 22/10 08:23:25 (System log: Kernel-Power 42/107, Power-Troubleshooter 1); `render._run_ffmpeg` counted the night with `time.monotonic()` (QPC includes sleep on Python 3.13), raised "quá thời gian chờ" 12 s before the audio mix ended, and `TemporaryDirectory` cleanup hit a still-held `expected.m4a`, masking the real error. Video 11 failed on Codex line 457: Whisper wrote "硬移反滑排滑" (likely 印尼反华排华); the batch answer is not kept, so whether Codex returned empty or Chinese is unknown.

**Sleep-safe limits (owner approved "1"):** `common.AwakeTime` + `SLEEP_GAP_S` 60: a poll gap longer than that is sleep, not work. Used by `render._run_ffmpeg` (limit unchanged), `subtitle_clean.clean_video` (5-min stall) and `speech_audio.apply_tempo` (120 s; added beyond the approved two steps, disclosed). `render._work_directory` replaces `TemporaryDirectory`: `_remove_tree` retries `CLEANUP_WAITS` (.2,.5,1,2,3 s) and never raises, so the render's own error surfaces. `render.clear_leftover_work(folder)` (called by `pipeline` before choosing the destination, on the job's own `render_directory`) removes only `.render-work-*` dirs and then the folder if empty, so a retry keeps the name instead of `_lan-2`. Tests `test_sleep_safe.py` (16); `test_render` timeout test now steps the clock 30 s at a time.

**Translation line repair (owner approved "2b"; invariant 8 exception):** `codex_translation._rejected_ids` finds lines whose text alone fails `_validate_response` in an otherwise valid answer; if 1..`REPAIR_LIMIT` (3), `_repair_lines` sends ONE follow-up with the same model/effort and `REPAIR_NOTE` appended after the versioned instruction (prompt versions unchanged), only those IDs, previous context up to the first bad line; a still-unusable answer raises "... Đã hỏi lại Codex một lần nhưng vẫn chưa được." Batch record gains `repair: {ids, metadata}` (validated by `_valid_repair` on reuse); report `calls` lists the repair call; review note per repaired line starts with `REPAIR_REASON`. Not approved/not done: keeping the rejected answer (2a). Tests `test_translation_repair.py` (12).

**One-box captions (owner: one box, square corners; "cách A"; "duyệt mục 1 và 1/6"):** new `caption_layout.py`: `wrap` (greedy + libass-like balancing, explicit `\N`), `place` (per axis `_axis`: text box at the usual place if the own caption fits inside, else the smallest box max(text, caption) covering it, text centred; usual place = caption rows' centre, picture centre), `layout`, `matched_size` (per video: own caption rows height in 288-units, >= chosen, <= 1.5x and 36; NOT per line — large per-line regions were plates/trays/bags, `research/grown-lines.png`), `PADDING` 1/6 line, `write_captions` (one ASS in picture pixels: Box drawing layer 0 + Caption `\an5\pos` layer 1, WrapStyle 2, braces/backslashes neutralised). `render._caption_boxes` replaces `_cover_events`; burned+box uses `captions.ass` only (no SRT force_style); plan `caption_cover` gains `layout: one_box`, `text_size`; `subtitle_style.size` stays the chosen size. `caption_cover._candidates` records per-row (count, first x, last x); `decide` uses `_caption_parts`: rows on the caption rows, plus dense (>= `DENSE_ROWS` x median) runs within `ROW_TOLERANCE` .3 line or >= `SECOND_LINE` .75 line tall; separate off-row candidates only if line-shaped. Measured on 6 real videos (3,129 lines, `research/analyse_v2.py`): regions past caption rows 7-48% -> 0-9%. Known miss kept: undetected captions (du-lich 19/10 video 10 line 476) still use the typical box. Tests: `test_caption_layout.py` (21, incl. real renders and the edge check that fails the old two-layer render: top edge 39.8% white), `test_caption_cover.py` +6.

**Verification:** offline replays `work/20261022-sleep-and-line-repair/repro-{old,new}.json`; mirror suites 1,291 tests with only the pre-existing `test_campaign_capture_freezes_mix_*` (also fails on untouched code; the campaign now captures current form values) and 4 tests needing the real `.venv` python; live suite after deploy in `work/20261022-single-caption-box/related-summary-live.json`. ## 19/10/2026 — Auto mode from campaigns: finished video → AI caption → auto-approve → auto-post (Claude Code; not yet deployed)

**Update 12 (21/10):** owner chose "cách A": `page_ai.auto_slots` rhythm runs `slot_start + k*slot_gap` to the end of each day (`points = max(videos_per_day, ceil((1440 - start_min)/gap))`), still at most `videos_per_day` videos per calendar date and never before `slot_start`; so a video ready after the day's first `videos_per_day` times goes out the same day. `validate_plan` unchanged (it already counts per date and only accepts `required_slots`). Panel text (`page_ai_ui.describe_slots`) explains the catch-up. No end-of-day hour (owner may ask for one later). Tests: `test_page_ai_auto.py` +4 (incl. the owner's 21/10 data: ready 12:33 -> 12:40, not 22/10 09:30). Real data at 15:48: next free times 15:55, 16:00, 16:05; video 8 stays armed at 22/10 09:30 (existing schedules are not moved). Developed in `work/20261021-same-day-catchup/stage/`, deployed 15:47 with no worker running; backups in `before/`.

**Update 11 (21/10):** smart caption cover, deployed 15:28 together with Update 10 (slow switch) while the campaign was paused (no worker process; checked). New `teodub/caption_cover.py`: one sequential PyAV decode of the picture source, frames analysed <= 960 px wide; per Vietnamese cue (output time mapped to source as `start_s + t*effective_speed`) 1–3 pairs of frames `PAIR_S`=0.2 s apart; caption pixels = luma >= 185 and >= 75 above the local minimum, kept only if present in both frames (static caption over moving background); long straight edges removed (1-D min/max window filters, `LONG_EDGE_HEIGHT` .16 H, `LONG_EDGE_WIDTH` .25 W); row runs taller than `TALL_BAND` .14 H are split with a higher row threshold; runs on a line merged across `WORD_GAP` .10 W only if word-like (`WORD_HEIGHT` .5 of the line, `WORD_DENSITY` .08); `learn_band` takes the mode of line centres (>= `MIN_LINES` 3 sightings) for caption rows + p90 width; `decide` keeps candidates in the band window (±1.3 lines), always covers the band rows, else the typical box. `render.render_video` (box on): text keeps `ass_box_style` (BorderStyle=3 snug box); `_cover_events` -> rectangles in `band.ass` per cue (only while the cue shows); any non-cancel exception -> old `subtitle_band.band_events` wide band, plan `caption_cover: {fallback: wide_band, error}`; plan `caption_cover` report otherwise. Real-video evaluation (`work/20261021-caption-cover/preview/`, `evaluate_stage.py`): du-lich-6 1252/1299 lines seen 105 s, cd13-1 333/346, cd13-3video-2 240/245, du-lich-4 298/300, an-uong-3 140/154. Also fixed: `campaign._source_retry_at()` returned None when only per-video failures existed (Update 8 changed `_all_unavailable`), so `resume_after` was missing and the 21/10 12:51 wait never resumed; now earliest paused `until`, else +`WAIT_FALLBACK_S` 30 min, and `resume_retry_failed` makes the UI resume with `--retry-failed`. Tests: `test_caption_cover.py` (8, synthetic libass captions incl. testsrc2 static edges, large lettering, spaced words, no captions), `test_render.py` (snug cover e2e + wide-band fallback), `test_campaign.py` (+2), `test_campaign_ui.py` (+1). Backups `work/20261021-caption-cover/before/`, `work/20261021-slow-switch/before/`.

**Update 10 (21/10):** slow-transfer switch in `browser_download.download_media(..., may_switch=False)`: `_check_speed()` keeps a deque of (monotonic time, bytes); after `SLOW_GRACE_S`=60 s and with a full `SLOW_WINDOW_S`=60 s window, a transfer whose remaining bytes would need more than `SLOW_REMAINING_S`=15 min at the window speed (or, without Content-Length, a speed below `SLOW_FLOOR_BPS`=300 KB/s) raises `MediaTransferError(reason='too_slow')`; the partial file is removed by the existing finally. `resolve_providers` passes `may_switch` only when a later provider is not paused in `ProviderHealth` (the last one always finishes). `too_slow` is per-transfer, not in `PAUSES`. Viện Phần Mềm's browser-download transfer is not covered. Developed and tested in `work/20261021-slow-switch/stage/` (a copy of `teodub/` + `tests/`) because a campaign was running; tests: `test_slow_switch.py` (9, fake clock + fake CDN response) and `test_download_adapter.py` fake transfer now takes `**kwargs`. Trigger: video 9 on 21/10, SaveTik link at ~0.45 MB/s for 8 min, then broke at 21%.

**Incident (21/10, 10:31):** my code edits for Update 9 landed (10:20–10:22) while the campaign was running; video 7's worker (started 10:00, old `subtitle_style` already imported) lazily imported the new `render`/`subtitle_band` at the render stage and failed with `ImportError: cannot import name 'SUBTITLE_BOXES'`. Recovery: "Thử lại video lỗi" resumes it at render (ASR/translation/voice saved). Rule added to CLAUDE.md: never edit `teodub/` while a job runs. Same session, video 8 failed in translation: Codex batch 2 (ids 301–600) answer rejected by `_validate_response` at id 556 ("在路边随便找个店都不会采雷", Whisper misheard 踩雷); the raw answer is not kept (temp dir). Retry resubmits batch 2 only (batch 1 kept).

**Update 9 (21/10):** caption box ("Khung che Sub gốc"). New persisted option `subtitle_box` in `subtitle_style.SUBTITLE_BOXES` (`none` default / `white` = white box black text / `black`), returned by `validate_subtitle_style` ONLY when burned and not `none`, so every old config/hash stays identical (`test_old_jobs_keep_selectable_caption_mode` unchanged). In `render.render_video(..., subtitle_box=)` the burned SRT path is unchanged when off; when on: (1) a separate script `work/band.ass` from `teodub/subtitle_band.py` (PlayRes = frame pixels, rectangles only, never caption text) drawn by a first `subtitles=` filter, (2) the same SRT with `ass_box_style` (BorderStyle=3 opaque box in the band colour, Outline = size/4 script units, same Arial/size/margins). Band geometry is derived from ffmpeg's SRT script units (384x288, MarginV 18, MarginL/R 22): line height = size*H/288, bottom = H - 18*H/288 + padding + 0.15 line, width 92%, height = predicted lines; `caption_lines()` predicts libass wrapping with Pillow + Windows Arial (9/9 sample captions matched libass on 1920x1080, 1280x720, 1080x1920; libass breaks on advance widths). Gaps <= 1 s keep one continuous band. Plan `subtitle_style` gains `box` only when on. UI: `app.py` box row (row 12; help label row 13, Drive row 14), colour picker disabled while a box is chosen; campaign `CONFIG_KEYS` + `describe_pipeline_settings` carry it. Tests: `test_subtitle_band.py` (13), `test_subtitle_style.py` (+2), `test_render.py` (+1 real FFmpeg: grey "own caption" bar hidden while spoken, visible after), `test_ui.py` (+2). Visual check on the owner's real source (still frames in the session scratchpad only). Backups `work/20261021-subtitle-box/before/`.

**Update 8 (21/10):** new first download source `douyin` ("Douyin trực tiếp"), `teodub/downloaders/douyin_direct.py`. It opens `https://www.douyin.com/video/<id>` once in the existing private headless context (no login, no cookies, no profile), observes the page's own `/aweme/v1/web/aweme/detail/?aweme_id=<id>` response (ignores other ids, non-200 answers are counted as refusals because the page re-asks by itself), `choose_stream()` picks highest height then bitrate among `video.bit_rate[].play_addr` URLs accepted by `is_public_media_url` (zjcdn/douyinvod) and `data_size <= MAX_BYTES`; transfer/validation are the unchanged `download_media` + `validate_downloaded_video`. Reasons: `refused` (403s + page moved to the feed; per-video, NOT remembered), `parse_failed` (filter_detail / no video id), `blocked` (whole-title verification page; `PAUSES['blocked']`=1 h, chain continues), `timeout`, `unsupported_media`. `_all_unavailable` now excludes sources that failed for this video only, so "Douyin refused + sites down" waits ("Chờ nguồn tải: chưa nguồn tải nào lấy được video này…") while "every source rejects this video" stays a real failure. Registry order: douyin, savetik, vienphanmem, socialvideo, anysave. ## 17/10/2026 — Facebook "Speak With People Directly" prompt left open after Publish (Claude Code; not yet deployed)

Evidence: 16:15:45 and 16:20:54 both recorded one Publish click (`unknown`, paused), the owner's screenshot at 16:23 showed Chrome on Reel settings with the "Speak With People Directly" dialog and a greyed Post, and the Page Reels tab at 16:31 had no new reel. Cause in `facebook_web_post.py`: "Not now" for the call prompt only ran inside the result-observer loop, which returns immediately when `result_checks_enabled` is false; additionally the dialog-name regex lacked "Speak with people directly" and the text test was case-sensitive ("Call now" vs "Call Now"), so it would not have matched even with checks on.

Fix (publisher submit boundary, approved by the user): new `_dismiss_call_prompt(tab, cancel, finished, progress, seconds)` polls up to `CALL_PROMPT_WAIT_SECONDS`=12 s after the single Publish click and presses only the dialog's single visible "Not now"/"Lúc khác"/"Maybe later" button when exactly one matching dialog is visible and its text matches `Gọi ngay|Call now` (case-insensitive). It never clicks Publish/Add Button, swallows non-cancel errors, and with checks off it waits here (before the pause return) up to that deadline; with checks on the observer loop calls the same helper with seconds=0 (no clock use, no wait: an early version that waited 12 s before observing broke 11 existing short-deadline tests, which pass on the original, and a clock-faking test also showed the helper must not call time.monotonic for a single look). Final: 1,329/1,329 OK across all test_page_*, test_facebook_*, campaign, AI, review, autopilot and UI modules (55 page/facebook modules included). `CALL_PROMPT_NAMES` gained "Speak with people directly". New `tests/test_page_call_prompt.py` (6 offline cases, incl. end-to-end with a delayed prompt for paused and checks-on modes; the two end-to-end tests fail on the backed-up original with `None != 1`). Not verified on real Facebook. Backups: `work/20261017-call-prompt/before/`. The two stuck attempts stay `Đã gửi — chưa xác minh`; no automatic resend. Needs safe restart.

## 15/10/2026 — Independent submission and publication verification

Browser posting separates the account's upload/composer slot from cooperative result observers; Playwright stays on its owning thread. After one Publish action, later approved posts may prepare and publish while earlier results are checked. Submitted attempts retain immutable tracking data separately from receipts. Startup resumes eligible result checks without requiring the Page window to be open. An explicit status recheck only observes the previous submission. Existing stopped/overdue unsubmitted drafts are not automatically armed or sent.

The schedule and UI distinguish submitted/verifying, verified published, and unverified results requiring attention. Confirmation requires submission-associated visible evidence, numeric Page ownership, full caption and positive matching video duration/aspect metadata. Receipt, post state and publication event commit atomically; the schedule reconciles durable confirmation even after a lost/error reply. Imported old tabs, competing indistinguishable attempts, foreign content and duplicate result claims cannot establish success. Insights URLs retain their exact opaque content_id; an unknown public permalink or actual publication time remains blank.

The related suite passed **1,062/1,062** in **520.354 seconds at 09:57:50 UTC+7**, no failures/errors/skips. This full checkpoint precedes late Insights layout changes. Later focused runs cover recognized Insights previews outside main and inside navigation, tiny structural groups, comment controls and caption expansion. Independent adversarial review reproduced and fixed borrowing an owner from another sidebar/card and ignoring an offscreen second player. All three adversarial cases now pass. The final cross-component check passed **42/42 at 10:16:44**, including the real offline browser test where B publishes before A is confirmed. Overlapping counts must not be added.

**Live verification is still pending.** Both actual previously submitted videos remain unverified after status-only rechecks. ## 14/10/2026 — Read-only reconciliation of stopped browser attempts

Added the visible **Kiểm tra lại trạng thái…** action in the Page schedule panel and a scoped confirmation dialog. It accepts an explicit direct HTTPS Facebook video permalink or one already-open result tab. The worker does not follow discovered notification/Insights references and cannot call prepare, upload or Publish. New unsubmitted drafts, active jobs and incomplete frozen video evidence remain ineligible. Closing the dialog stops its UI polling; an already authorized inspection may finish in the background within its 90-second request budget. Application/session shutdown and deadline cancellation are checked before commit.

The inspector requires one primary player, exact full caption, numeric Page ownership, frozen positive video duration (within two seconds), native aspect ratio and old-attempt association. Hidden/comment/editor/navigation content and foreign/recommended players cannot supply the receipt. A visible exact publication time must identify the selected attempt; without time, only one historical submitted attempt with matching Page/full body/media SHA plus the user's explicit direct-link confirmation qualifies. Missing metadata/expanded text or ambiguity stays unverified. Existing prepare/publish methods and pre-existing scripts are AST-identical to the previous receipt/queue patch.

`page_browser_reconcile.py` captures schedule/post/events/target/history, checks immutable media SHA, locks schedule then post, revalidates the capture and commits the receipt/post/schedule/event/journal in one attached DELETE-journal SQLite transaction. Stale approval, hash/registry changes, duplicate external URLs, conflicting receipts, WAL, write failures and cancellation fail without partial state. Removed schedules remain removed; IDs, due times, source reservations, video/body and old events stay intact. Rechecking an already verified identical result preserves its original receipt. An unknown actual publication date remains blank; receipt.checked_at/event.at hold the verification time. No reset/replay/arming occurs. UI uses contextual **Chưa xác minh đã đăng** and header **Đã xác nhận đăng / Đã lưu, chưa bật** without changing persisted state keys or status API fields.

Request integration rejects malformed nested previews and IDs before adding a queued request; wrong-scope inspection envelopes cancel only their request; malformed reply IDs cannot stop the desktop polling loop. Passive preflight avoids hashing twice in Tk; final commit still hashes and rereads the DOM/authentication/URL after hashing. Existing publisher/queue, API, AI and web operations retain their behavior.

Final related regression **988/988 PASS in 347.074 s at 23:04:12**, no failures/errors/skips. The 89 new cases comprise 31 storage, 20 DOM, 17 request integration and 21 UI cases. Earlier focused suites overlap and must not be summed. The legacy combined Tk suite still emits teardown `invalid command name ...poll` warnings; its final unittest result is green. Nine source files and four test files compile; exact diff/hashes and the AST audit are in `work/20261014-browser-status-reconcile/verification.json` and sibling artifacts.

## 10/10/2026 — Bật lịch hàng loạt và tiếp tục đợt kế hoạch

Latest request: enable all or selected Page schedules and explain continuing after six-day trial. Desktop PageSchedulePanel now extended selection (Ctrl/Shift, Chọn tất cả), Bật phần đã chọn, Bật tất cả (current account/Page only), and Tạm dừng phần đã chọn. Poll retains entire selection, focus and scroll. Single-only actions (reschedule/open/cancel) disabled for multiselect and selected() rejects multiple to avoid silently acting on first row. Bật tất cả reloads Page list; repeated clicks retain existing active jobs. No real user schedules armed or changed by this implementation.

PageScheduleController.start_many checks each durable record against exact account/Page, deduplicates IDs, arms only unattempted eligible future jobs through existing start/locks; skips past/attempted/completed/cancelled/other Page and reports per-item outcome/reason. An error on one job does not stop other eligible jobs. No catchup, auto-reschedule, time/body/media/target changes or browser/AI call. Kết quả bật lịch opens the latest full per-row report, preserved despite polling. Main desktop/Chrome/Codex not restarted; reopen desktop only when idle to load changes. No web files changed/deploy needed; these bulk buttons are desktop UI.

Existing AI policy supports days=6 / interval_days=6; added in-app instructions in Đăng theo kế hoạch AI. Set first call after current trial, selected campaign sources, enable AI and optionally auto-publish, save. Repeat uses fresh context excluding videos already in schedules/plans, cannot replay old posts, preserves saved model/effort and max1 AI call/Page/day. If auto-publish off, new plan still needs explicit approval. Added test for six-day recurring claim and exclusion of prior plan video; no real AI calls. Does not automatically configure user's live AI policy or guarantee browser video/Reels wizard support. Backup and verification at work/20261010-page-bulk-schedules.

## 09/10/2026 — Đăng theo kế hoạch AI khi đến giờ, nguồn chiến dịch, lịch sử

Latest user requested the per-Page small window: select campaign sources, send data to AI to analyze/create times/captions, automatically schedule/publish according to saved opt-in policy, journal interactions. Implemented `teodub/page_ai.py` (SQLite policies/runs/journal, due-only controller) and `page_ai_ui.py` (seventh PageManager tab **Đăng theo kế hoạch AI**, source multiselect, cadence, model/effort, future due, interval0 oneoff or >=horizon, opt-in auto-publish, history). Default suggestions 2 videos/day + 3 text/week, 7-day plans, gpt-5.6-terra/low; **no production Page policy enabled**. Existing linked Page/chat and existing proposed plan/schedules preserved.

Important honest boundary: new AI calls are isolated `codex exec` via the existing authenticated `_invoke_codex`/JSON schema/read-only tools-disabled engine. It does NOT send to/resume the user's linked planning chat. `codex_chat.py` stays read-only. No full private browser scraping/view/reach collection. AI context<=45000 chars, max1 call/Page/day, explicit saved model/effort, no fallback/retry. Waiting/polling is read-only, no inference. No account API keys copied/paid API.

Singleton existing PageCommandBridge owns execution; recovery running->needs_attention and disables same revision; >10min missed due and second daily appointment disable and journal. Revise/pause/Page/chat change blocks old response, closes own worker on cancellation; never auto replay after a crash. Auto opt-in validates exact Page, selected source/hash/SRT, plan UUID, cadence, date window and 30min separation, passive stage worker then approve/arm on Tk thread; disabled auto leaves passive proposed plan. Schedule source_kind scheduled_ai/source_run_id persisted, UI says isolated AI result honestly; never call a synthetic Teo ID an observed Codex turn. Journals watch actual scheduler state changes once; Facebook published only with existing receipt verification. Tạm dừng AI does not pause already armed Facebook schedules.

Web: new page_ai.html/js, `save_ai_policy`/`pause_ai_policy` mailbox commands admin/CSRF, binds target and linked thread at submit+execution; server never opens browser or invokes AI. Public snapshot whitelist contains campaign labels/counts and each Page's config/run summaries/latest12 history entries (details capped16000); desktop has full history. Same source/model/cadence limits validated at web/mailbox and disk existence on desktop. PageManager geometry1160x880 to fit new tab. README explains usage/limits.

No actual AI inference, new real policy/plan approval, upload or FB post. Work dir `work/20261009-page-ai-autopilot`, backups/before, deployment.json. User must reopen Tèo when idle for new desktop imports/tab/controller; web is already updated.

## Mới nhất: ADAM theo mẫu chạy miễn phí trên máy

User chọn miễn phí cục bộ sau khi được hỏi ADAM bản gốc/CapCut/theo mẫu. Đã làm phần nhập/lưu/chọn ADAM theo mẫu và nghe thử bằng pipeline local hiện tại. **Chưa có mẫu ADAM thật; chưa tạo hoặc nghe đánh giá giọng ADAM thật.** Đã nhờ user gửi/chọn đoạn mẫu rõ 3–8 giây. Không tải demo ElevenLabs, gọi API, dùng token/dịch vụ giọng trả phí hoặc đổi tên một preset khác thành ADAM.

`adam_voice.py`: LABEL ADAM (theo mẫu), import_reference dùng prepare_reference hiện có để lấy tối đa 8 giây đầu, mono48kPCM16, kiểm thời lượng/RMS/metadata/hash. Lưu version UUID trong data/voice-library/adam/UUID/reference.wav + reference.json; selected.json atomic chỉ pointer phiên bản hiện tại. Không ghi đè mẫu của job cũ. checked_reference kiểm vị trí hồ sơ/path + validate_reference; bản copy tự đủ dùng dù nguồn ngoài bị xóa. Mẫu lỗi/cancel không đổi selected pointer; không tạo default giả. AdamImportDialog đọc/chuẩn hóa trên backgroundthread, queue Tk, close/Destroy cancel và dừng timer, lỗi giữ dialog cho retry, callback chọn mẫu trên mainthread.

App: nút **Nhập mẫu ADAM…** bên hàng nghe thử; alias ADAM luôn có để dễ tìm, thiếu mẫu có chú thích và chặn preview/full, không fallback. self.adam_reference được load từ thư viện khi mở; reference_aliases ghép với preset cũ, voice_selection chuyển thành Giọng từ mẫu + absolute reference_audio + frozen SHA như engine hiện có. Không đổi TTS provider, model IDs/cache schema hoặc campaign allowlist. Turbo/Nano, tốc độ/reading_style/Nghe lại dùng đúng sample path/hash. apply_adam chọn local/alias rồi lưu prefs. fill_profile phiên cũ giữ đúng version/path, kể cả missing/corrupt (không đổi giọng ngầm), new work kiểm lại hồ sơ trước chạy. Campaign capture_settings lấy cấu hình giọng/path/hash tương tự, summary có ADAM; dùng Lấy cấu hình từ Video đơn cho chiến dịch mới. Import/apply bị chặn khi app/campaign bận.

**157 kiểm tra đạt, 22,121 giây**, không skip/lỗi: tests.test_adam_voice (15 mới), test_voice_profiles, test_ui, test_speech, test_ai_models, test_campaign_ui. Sau bổ sung kiểm cấu trúc metadata chạy lại 15 Adam tests: đạt 2,565 giây. Test âm thanh tổng hợp tạo tại tempdir; real local normalization/validation, fake model/worker, Tk ẩn. Kiểm chọn/tạo cấu hình preview/full/campaign, model infer nhận ref_audio đúng nguồn, cache không infer lần nữa, phiên bản cũ không đổi, mẫu bị xóa/tamper, quá ngắn/cancel, prefs restore, busy/dialog lỗi/queue. Lượt thử inference ban đầu bị global Popen fixture chặn ffmpeg tempo; test chuyển legacy nhịp để tập trung đường reference, không sửa production tempo. Không mạng/ASR/dịch/model download/inference thật, không sửa userdata/đóng hoặc mở lại Tèo/worker. README hướng dẫn nhập và giới hạn độ giống; user mở lại app khi nghỉ để có nút mới.

## Mới nhất: Video đơn tách tải → dịch Sub → sửa lời → lồng tiếng

User yêu cầu nút tải video riêng, tải + dịch Sub riêng và bước cuối lồng tiếng, giữ tùy chọn auto cũ. Đã đặt bốn nút dưới ô input: **1. Tải video**, **2. Tải video và dịch Sub**, **3. Xem / sửa lời thoại**, **4. Lồng tiếng và xuất video**. Nút Bắt đầu cũ đổi nhãn **Tự động toàn bộ**, giữ full flow. Stagebuttons bị khóa cùng app/campaign busy. Nút xem lời không còn tự bắt đầu dịch hoặc tự mở sau dịch; thiếu lời thì chỉ hướng dẫn chạy bước 2. Bước 2 có thể chạy trực tiếp từ link hoặc dùng local đã tải, luôn dịch video mới theo lựa chọn model (không dùng ô SRT), chưa tạo giọng/render/Drive. Sau chuẩn bị dừng chờ user.

Pipeline mode=download chỉ acquire/probe, trả outputs.source, không validate/chạy model/CapCut/ASR/TTS/render/Drive. Tiếp tục dùng worker lock + state cũ như trước. App `use_prepared_result` chọn video local sau download/captions, download bỏ lựa chọn SRT cũ khỏi form nhưng giữ tệp, captions chọn raw SRT đã kiểm SHA. Bước 4 require_subtitles=True chốt vào config full; thiếu SRT bị chặn cả GUI và pipeline, không âm thầm gửi dịch. Nút auto full không mang cờ nên vẫn dịch khi chưa có SRT. Worker caption không cần mẫu giọng/Drive/output; download không cần model hợp lệ. Mở kết quả hiểu outputs.source/subtitles để mở đúng thư mục.

`dialogue_editor.draft_binding` nhận thêm đúng runs/UUID/captions/vietnamese.srt, đọc state, kiểm path/SHA và trả source SHA. Nhờ đó dùng raw Sub vừa dịch để lồng tiếng không cần mở editor/lưu lại; vẫn chặn dùng nhầm video hoặc raw bị sửa. Prepared artifacts được phục hồi khi last-job download/captions completed, không gọi worker/mạng. Preferences selection_job gắn lựa chọn SRT chỉnh sửa với job để giữ bản sửa qua restore nhưng không đè SRT của phiên mới bằng bản cũ; preview giữ form prepared từ prefs. Immutable config/state/outputs phiên cũ không sửa. README/help đã ghi cách chạy từng bước/auto.

**107 kiểm tra đạt, 18,611 giây, không skip/lỗi**: tests.test_single_steps (13 mới), test_dialogue_editor (14 cập nhật hành vi button mới), test_pipeline, test_google_drive_app_ui, test_campaign_app_shell, test_campaign_ui. Kiểm download-only không inference/upload và resume không tải lại, download→caption→dub truyền đúng nguồn/lời không dịch/tải lại, stop/resume, final thiếu Sub không gọi AI, model/voice/Drive không cản download, SRT raw sourcebinding/tamper, không auto bước kế tiếp, restore/malformed state, busy controls, mở thư mục và bốn nút vừa bề ngang 1000px. Lượt đầu 106 đạt; bổ sung case metadata hỏng + restore KeyError và bộ cuối 107 đạt. Toàn bộ fake engines/workers và tempdata/Tk ẩn, không tải/dịch/lồng tiếng thật hoặc tác động app/userdata đang chạy. Không đóng/restart app/worker. User mở lại Tèo khi công việc nghỉ để nạp nút mới.

## Mới nhất: xem và sửa lời thoại Việt trong Video đơn

User yêu cầu xem trước/sửa văn phong trong phần Video đơn. Nút **Xem / sửa lời thoại** ở hàng Nghe thử giọng: có SRT thì mở trực tiếp; chưa có SRT thì đọc captions đã kiểm hash của phiên full/captions cùng input hoặc cùng source.path; không có bản phù hợp thì chạy worker mode=captions (tải/nhận dạng/dịch), kết thúc trước mọi TTS/preflight/render/Drive. Worker xong mở editor, khi đóng app không tự mở. Mở lại app bấm nút để mở bản lời đã chuẩn bị. Nút Bắt đầu cũ vẫn chạy full tự động như trước; không buộc duyệt mọi video/chiến dịch.

`teodub/dialogue_editor.py`: bảng câu/mốc gốc/lời Việt, ô sửa từng câu, Câu trước/Câu tiếp, lưu luôn cả câu cuối chưa chuyển, cảnh báo đóng khi có thay đổi. Save tạo SRT UTF-8 mới trong data/dialogue-drafts/UUID/loi-thoai.srt và sidecar SHA/source SHA; giữ thứ tự và mốc, không ghi đè raw translation, cache, video hay config phiên cũ. Chặn rỗng/chữ Trung/sai số câu và nguồn SRT bị sửa trong lúc editor mở. Lỗi giữ editor/nội dung. Mốc không chỉnh trong bản này; bảng Từ ngữ nếu bật vẫn được áp dụng ở lượt full, có giải thích trên editor.

`app.py`: lưu chọn SRT mới vào form/preferences, nguồn đã tải trong phiên được dùng lại bằng local path. Bản chọn đã sửa được phục hồi sau last-job để không bị bản config cũ đè mất. Full job mới chốt subtitles_sha256 và managed dialogue_source_sha256; pipeline kiểm trước TTS, chặn dùng lời đã sửa cho nguồn khác hoặc SRT đổi sau start. Không đổi immutable request/cache của phiên cũ; full mới dùng giọng/tốc độ/style hiện tại, không gửi dịch lại khi có SRT. `mode=captions` không cần chọn mẫu giọng/Drive/output hợp lệ; chỉ cần source, thư mục gốc, lựa chọn model. Resume captions completed kiểm hash và dùng lại lời, không gọi lại engines. Nút khóa khi app/chiến dịch bận; save cũng kiểm busy.

Kiểm **94 đạt, 16,138 giây**, không skip/lỗi: tests.test_dialogue_editor (14 mới), test_pipeline, test_google_drive_app_ui, test_campaign_app_shell, test_campaign_ui. Gồm pipeline chuẩn bị→sửa→full xác minh văn bản tới TTS/render, không ASR/dịch lại; không TTS/preflight/render/Drive ở bước chuẩn bị; chặn wrong-source/tampered SRT; hidden Tk kiểm lưu câu cuối/chuyển câu/lỗi giữ nội dung/hủy đóng, tự mở sau chuẩn bị, không dùng lời phiên khác, restore draft và busy. Chỉ mocks/tempdata/Tk ẩn, không tạo giọng thật/dịch thật/Drive/network, không sửa dữ liệu user, không đóng/restart app/worker. User mở lại Tèo khi công việc nghỉ để nạp nút mới.

## Mới nhất: kết nối Google Drive và tự lưu video thành phẩm

User yêu cầu kết nối tài khoản Google Drive, tick tùy chọn để tự lưu video thành phẩm. Đã triển khai backend, GUI và tích hợp cả Video đơn/chiến dịch trong dự án này. Chỉ sửa source/tests/docs/work mới; không đóng/restart app hoặc worker, không sửa tool CapCut cũ, cấu hình Codex hoặc video/tài khoản/chiến dịch thật. **Chưa kết nối Google thật hoặc upload video thật.** Không dùng Google Drive connector của Codex để cấp quyền cho app độc lập.

`teodub/google_drive.py`: requests đã có trong requirements, không thêm dependency. DriveClient.accounts/connect/disconnect/folders/create_folder/upload_video/upload_status + normalize_drive_config nhận payload direct hoặc nested drive_upload, thiếu nghĩa OFF. Official OAuth Desktop: system browser, loopback 127.0.0.1 random port, state/PKCE, timeout300s, offline refresh, scope drive.file. Strict Google endpoints/no redirects, client JSON Desktop được chọn tại máy, không đọc profile/cookies/mật khẩu. Token + client data và resumable-session capability được mã hóa DPAPI theo Windows user/account; registry chỉ public name/email/ID/connected và opaque refs, không khóa plaintext. Account ID namespace theo clientID+permissionID, epoch kiểm trước/sau HTTP và trước lưu refresh; disconnect đổi generation/chặn yêu cầu tiếp theo. Request đã gửi trước disconnect vẫn có thể được server xử lý, nên giữ journal để reconcile.

Upload streaming 8MiB chunks, hash SHA+MD5 actualbytes trước mạng và kiểm stat, tên video giữ nguyên. Journal khóa theo source+hash+account/folder, generateIds và lưu ID trước POST tạo tệp. Timeout/gián đoạn giữ ID, resume GET tệp theo ID trước; offset/resumable-status probe, expiredsession chỉ khởi tạo lại cùng ID. SessionLocation chỉ Google URL được chấp nhận và lưu encryptedref. Hoàn tất chỉ sau metadata remote khớp ID, size/checksum, MIME, appProperties/key/SHA, folderparent và không trashed. Cached receipt cần confirmed_remote khớp; không tự tạo bản khác hoặc đổi account/folder khi lỗi. Folder ID thường phải trùng target; root alias nhận actualrootID, không dùng phản hồi folder khác. Không chia sẻ/public file hoặc thay quyền. drive.file chỉ app-created/app-authorized; chưa GooglePicker cho mọi folder cũ, SharedDrive hoặc scanallDrive.

`teodub/google_drive_ui.py`: configure_google_drive(parent,data_dir,initial,on_save) mở modal localaccounts + checkbox tự lưu + chọn clientJSON + kết nối Google + tải/chọn/tạo folder + disconnect. Network/OAuth chạy background, progress/lỗi queue vào Tk mainthread, close/parentdestroy cancel và bỏ late result. Account/folder chọn theoID, recheck connected trước save. Root Studio thêm checkbox/nút và summary vào Video đơn, lưu canonical drive_upload trong preferences; config full chốt drive_upload+drive_data_dir, preview không gửi Drive. Restore preferences riêng cho video mới, resume dùng config cũ bất biến, không tự enable/upload quá khứ. Lỗi tải chỉ thêm thông báo riêng; localcompleted nhưng Drive chưa confirmed thì Continue được bật.

`teodub/drive_delivery.py`: upload_completed_video(config,result,progress,cancel) chỉ nhận full/completed/MP4-MOV có rendered.outputs khớp result, digest SHA đã lưu, path dưới output_dir và job trong data root; source_id là relativejobpath nên campaign/video không đụng nhau. Không tự refresh hash để nhận tệp đã bị đổi. job/drive-upload.json chỉ trạng thái delivery, pinned account/folder/source/hash; URL success canonical GoogleDrive fileID. Unknown network outcome khác failed, authfailure yêu cầu reconnect, cancel riêng. Adapter is_set hỗ trợ campaign stop Path và _StopRequests local+batch. Passive summary không tạo client/mạng/files, uploaded phải khớp source/hash/account/folder/fileID/link. Lỗi delivery/status/progress không làm mất localrender hoặc chuyển completed thànhfailed.

`pipeline.run` lưu completed result và rendered/checkpoints trước upload, events google_drive không có numeric render-percent (cloudprogress riêng), nested result.google_drive, không đổi rendered/cache/request. Cloudfail không gây rerender. `campaign` thêm drive_upload/drive_data_dir vào allowlist/fingerprint/snapshot; completed DONE resume kiểm outputs/hashes rồi chỉ delivery bằng frozen jobconfig, không gọi runner hoặc sửa completiontime/processingseconds. review_required không auto-upload. Kết quả cloud giữ riêng item.google_drive, không tăng errorcounter lồng tiếng. `campaign_ui` checkbox/nút cho chiến dịch mới, cột GoogleDrive sau posting, refresh một giây chỉ đọc localstatus, chi tiết có lỗi/link. Capture_settings atomic, giữ settings cũ và block tạo khi tick chưa kết nối; _create kiểm sharedcheckbox hiện tại để không âm thầm bỏ yêu cầu tự lưu. Manifest/config chiến dịch cũ không được sửa khi đổi lựa chọn.

**319 kiểm tra: 318 đạt, 1 skip, không lỗi, 65,027 giây**. Log `work/google-drive-auto-upload-tests.log`, hồ sơ `work/google-drive-auto-upload-verification.json`. 106 tests mới: backend36, modalTk24, app/campaignTk16, pipeline/campaigndelivery30. Hồi quy Pipeline/CampaignTab/management/library/batch/controller/tracking/app-shell/AI models/Facebook scheduling/posting. Skip test_symlink_campaign_and_queue_json_are_rejected do Windows thiếu quyền symlink (WinError1314), không phải GoogleDrive case. Google API/browser/engines/worker giả lập và dữ liệu TemporaryDirectory; có loopback OAuth callback localhost và Windows DPAPI với fakecredential thật.
